Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! This week we’re looking at a draft from a [**Magic Online**](https://web.archive.org/web/20221007121403/https://magic.wizards.com/en/articles/archive/2009-06-29) PTQ Top 8 for Pro Tour *Dark Ascension*.

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/mtg/images/daily/activity/da893_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20120201000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mtgo3076603)

## top 8 bracket

### Quaterfinals

**bradyoo**

smogon

**DGC**

Just4myrage

**Wirecat**

Sleepless

**Royal**

Hankyu

### Semi-finals

**bradyoo, 2-0**

DGC, 2-1

**Wirecat, 2-0**

Royal, 2-1

### Finals

**bradyoo, 2-1**

Wirecat, 2-0

### Champion

**bradyoo, 2-1**
